Corporate Profile
公司簡介


Sunny Optical Technology (Group) Company Limited (the “Company”                 舜 宇 光 學 科 技（ 集 團 ）有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」或
or “Sunny Optical Technology”, together with its subsidiaries, the              「舜宇光學科技」，連同其附屬公司「本集團」）
“Group”) (Stock Code: 2382.HK) is a global leading manufacturer of              （股份代號：2382.HK），是全球領先的大規模
integrated optical products with large-scale and a globally competitive         綜合光學產品製造商及具備國際競爭力的智能
provider of intelligent optical system solutions (whole device). The            光學系統解決方案（整機）提供商。本集團從事
Group is engaged in the design, research and development (“R&D”),               設計、研究與開發（「研發」）、生產及銷售綜合
production and sales of integrated optical products and intelligent             光學產品及智能光學系統解決方案（整機），並
optical system solutions (whole device). By deeply integrating cutting-         深度融合光學、電子、算法、軟件、機械及人
edge technologies such as optics, electronics, algorithms, software,            工智能（「AI」）等前沿技術，助力全球客戶在智
mechanics, and artificial intelligence (“AI”), the Group empowers global        能手機、汽車、擴展現實（「XR」）、泛物聯網
clients to achieve innovative breakthroughs and value growth across             （「泛IoT」）及光互連等應用領域實現創新突破
application fields including smartphones, vehicles, extended reality            及價值增長。
(“XR”), pan Internet of things (“Pan-IoT”) and optical interconnection.

macro environment, industry chain resources concentrated further         端交互入口的內生增長動能，是把握產業週期

and (D) other products.                                                    (D)其他產品。

      so as to accommodate two major XR display technology paths,                   領先的小型化封裝技術，本集團進一步實

RESTRICTED SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                             限制性股份獎勵計劃

On 22 March 2010 (the “Adoption Date”), the Board adopted                 於二零一零年三月二十二日（「採納日期」），董
the Restricted Share Award Scheme. Pursuant to the Restricted             事會採納限制性股份獎勵計劃。根據限制性股
Share Award Scheme, the Directors, employees, officers, agents            份獎勵計劃，本公司及其附屬公司之董事、僱
and consultants of the Company and its subsidiaries are entitled to       員、高級職員、代理及顧問均有權參與是項計
participate in this scheme.                                               劃。


The purposes of the Restricted Share Award Scheme are to assist the       限制性股份獎勵計劃之目的為協助本公司吸納
Company in attracting new staff as well as motivating and retaining its   新人、激勵及挽留現有人才。
existing talents.


The Restricted Share Award Scheme shall be effective from the             限制性股份獎勵計劃由採納日期起生效，並持
Adoption Date and shall continue in effect for a term of 10 years and     續生效十年，由其管理委員會（「管理委員會」）
be managed by the administration committee (the “Administration           及本公司限制性股份獎勵計劃的受託人管理。
Committee”) and the trustee of the Restricted Share Award Scheme          於二零二零年三月十六日，董事會決議將限制
of the Company. On 16 March 2020, the Board resolved to extend            性股份獎勵計劃的期限延長十年。因此，本應
the period of the Restricted Share Award Scheme for another ten           於二零二零年三月二十一日終止的限制性股份
years. As a result, the Restricted Share Award Scheme, which              獎勵計劃將於二零三零年三月二十一日終止，
should have originally terminated on 21 March 2020, shall, unless         除非經董事會決議案提前終止。限制性股份獎
terminated earlier by a resolution of the Board, terminate on 21 March    勵計劃的剩餘期限約為四(4)年。
2030. The remaining life of the Restricted Share Award Scheme was
approximately four (4) years.


The Administration Committee consists of members of the                   管 理 委 員 會 由 董 事 會 薪 酬 委 員 會（「 薪 酬 委 員
remuneration committee of the Board (the “Remuneration                    會 」）成 員 ， 獨 立 非 執 行 董 事 及 本 公 司 高 級 管
Committee”), independent non-executive Directors and senior               理層組成，董事會授予其管理限制性股份獎勵
Management of the Company, delegated with the power and authority         計劃的權力，包括根據上市規則第十七章審閱
by the Board to administer the Restricted Share Award Scheme,             及 ╱ 或批准與限制性股份獎勵計劃有關的事
including reviewing and/or approving matters relating to the Restricted   項。

2026 SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                           二零二六年股份獎勵計劃

Reference is made to the announcement of the Company dated 20                     按本公司於二零二六年三月二十日之公告，於
March 2026. On 18 March 2026, the Board has resolved to adopt the                 二零二六年三月十八日，董事會已決議採納本
2026 share award scheme of the Company (the “2026 Share Award                     公司的二零二六年股份獎勵計劃（「二零二六年
Scheme”).                                                                         股份獎勵計劃」）。


The purposes of the 2026 Share Award Scheme are to:                               二零二六年股份獎勵計劃旨在：


(a) provide the grantees under the 2026 Share Award Scheme (the                   (a) 為二零二六年股份獎勵計劃項下的獲授人
      “Selected Participant(s)”) with an opportunity to acquire a                  （「特選參與者」）提供獲取本公司所有權權
      proprietary interest in the Company;                                          益的機會；


(b) encourage and retain Selected Participants to work with the                   (b) 鼓勵及挽留特選參與者為本公司服務；
      Company;


(c) provide additional incentives to Selected Participants to achieve             (c) 為特選參與者提供達成績效目標的額外獎
      performance goals, with a view to achieving the objectives of                 勵，從而實現提升本公司價值的目標；及
      increasing the value of the Company; and


(d) align the interests of the Selected Participants directly to the              (d) 通過持有本公司股份，使特選參與者的利
      Shareholders through ownership of Shares in the Company.                      益直接與本公司股東的利益保持一致。


The Selected Participants are categorised into two groups, namely                 特選參與者分為兩類，分別是(i)僱員參與者（即
(i) employee participants (i.e., Directors and employees of the Group,            本集團董事及僱員，包括已獲得授出獎勵以作
including those who have been granted with awards as an inducement                為加入本公司之誘因的人士）；及(ii)服務提供者
to join the Company); and (ii) service provider participants (i.e.,               參與者（即在本集團日常業務過程中按持續或
independent consultants, suppliers, agents, and contractors who                   經常性基準向本集團提供服務的獨立顧問、供
provide services to the Group on a continuous or recurring basis in its           應商、代理商及承建商，該等服務符合本集團
ordinary course of business, which are in the interests of the Group’s            長期增長的利益）。
long-term growth).

“Group”) set out on pages 49 to 110, which comprise the condensed         括截至二零二六年六月三十日的簡明綜合財務
consolidated statement of financial position as of 30 June 2026 and       狀況表與截至該日止六個月期間的相關簡明綜

of changes in equity and condensed consolidated statement of              表附註。香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規
cash flows for the six-month period then ended, and notes to the          則規定，就中期財務資料編製的報告須符合其
condensed consolidated financial statements. The Rules Governing          中有關條文以及香港會計師公會（「香港會計師
the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited      公會」）頒佈的香港會計準則第34號（「香港會計
require the preparation of a report on interim financial information to   準則第34 號」）

be in compliance with the relevant provisions thereof and Hong Kong       負責根據香港會計準則第34號編製及呈列該等

as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants      該等簡明綜合財務報表作出結論，並按照委聘
(the “HKICPA”). The directors of the Company are responsible for          的協定條款僅向作為實體的          閣下報告結論，
the preparation and presentation of these condensed consolidated          且並無其他目的。我們不會就本報告的內容向

Scope of Review                                                                  審閱範圍

We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard on                 我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審閱委
Review Engagements 2410 “Review of Interim Financial Information                 聘準則第2410 號「由實體獨立核數師審閱中期
Performed by the Independent Auditor of the Entity” as issued by                 財務資料」進行審閱。審閱該等簡明綜合財務
the HKICPA. A review of these condensed consolidated financial                   報表包括主要向負責財務和會計事務的人員作
statements consists of making inquiries, primarily of persons                    出查詢，並應用分析性和其他審閱程序。審閱
responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying                   範圍遠小於根據香港核數準則進行審核的範
analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially less           圍，故不能令我們保證我們將知悉於審核中可
in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with Hong Kong                    能發現的所有重大事項。因此，我們不會發表
Standards on Auditing and consequently does not enable us to obtain              審核意見。
assurance that we would become aware of all significant matters that
might be identified in an audit. Accordingly, we do not express an
audit opinion.


Conclusion                                                                       結論

Based on our review, nothing has come to our attention that causes               按照我們的審閱，我們並無發現任何事項，令
us to believe that the condensed consolidated financial statements are           我們相信簡明綜合財務報表在各重大方面未根
not prepared, in all material respects, in accordance with HKAS 34.              據香港會計準則第34號編製。

      where the annual effective income tax rates are estimated to                 年度實際所得稅率估計低於15%。因此，
      be below 15 per cent. Therefore, a top-up tax is accrued in the              於本中期期間，根據本年度預估調整後涵

      adjusted covered taxes and net globe income for the year. The                至二零二六年六月三十日止六個月，本集

      expected to be levied on relevant group entities.                            集團實體進行徵收。

20. IMPAIRMENT ASSESSMENT ON                                                      20. 按預期信貸虧損模式計算之
    TRADE RECEIVABLES SUBJECT TO                                                      貿易應收款項的減值評估
    ECL MODEL

      As part of the Group’s credit risk management, except for the                作為本集團信貸風險管理的一部分，除出
      debtors with credit-impaired the Group uses debtors’ aging to                現信貸減值的應收款項外，本集團採用應
      assess the impairment for its customers which are with common                收款項的賬齡評估客戶減值，該等客戶具
      risk characteristics that are representative of the customers’               有共同風險特徵，即能代表客戶根據合約
      abilities to pay all amounts due in accordance with the contractual          條款支付所有到期款項的能力。本集團對

31. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF                                                 31. 金融工具的公允值計量
    FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS

   Fair value measurements and valuation                                        公允值計量及估值過程
   processes

   The Group’s management is responsible for the fair value                     本集團管理層負責財務報告所需資產及負
   measurements of assets and liabilities required for financial                債的公允值計量。管理層直接向董事會報
   reporting purposes. The management reports directly to the board             告該等公允值計量。
   of directors for these fair value measurements.


   In estimating the fair value, the Group uses market-observable               本集團於可得範圍內使用可觀察的市場數
   data to the extent it is available. For instruments with significant         據估計公允值。對於第三級下具有重大不
   unobservable inputs under Level 3, the Group will normally                   可觀察輸入數據的工具，本集團通常會聘
   engage external valuation experts with the recognised professional           請具有認可專業資格的外部估值專家進行
   qualifications to perform the valuations.                                    估值。

31. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF                                               31. 金融工具的公允值計量
    FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (Continued)                                          （續）

  Fair value measurements and valuation                                       公允值計量及估值過程（續）
  processes (Continued)

  The fair values of these financial assets and financial liabilities are     該等金融資產及金融負債的公允值（尤其是
  determined (in particular, the valuation technique(s) and inputs            所用的估值方法及輸入數據）及公允值計量
  used), as well as the level of the fair value hierarchy into which the      所屬的公允值層級（第一至三級）乃基於公
  fair value measurements are categorised (Levels 1 to 3) based on            允值計量輸入數據的可觀察程度而釐定。
  the degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements is
  observable.


  •    Level 1 fair value measurements are based on quoted prices             •   第一級公允值計量基於實體在計量日
       (unadjusted) in active market for identical assets or liabilities          可獲取相同資產或負債在活躍市場中
       that the entity can access at the measurement date;                        的報價（未經調整）；


  •    Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from                 •   第二級公允值計量乃除第一級計入的
       inputs other than quoted prices included within Level 1 that               報價外，自資產或負債的可觀察輸入
       are observable for the asset or liability, either directly (i.e. as        數據直接（即價格）或間接（即自價格
       prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from prices); and                      衍生）得出；及


  •    Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from                 •   第三級公允值計量乃自包括對資產或
       valuation techniques that include the lowest level inputs which            負債的公允值計量而言屬重大且並非
       are significant to the fair value measurement for the asset                基於可觀察市場數據的最低水平輸入
       or liability that are not based on observable market data                  數據（重大不可觀察輸入數據）的估值
       (significant unobservable inputs).                                         方法得出。
